specific, and realistic timeframes for tasks that help move projects forward. Here's a step-by-step guide to help you set actionable deadlines:\n\n1. **Break down the project**: Divide the project into smaller, manageable tasks or milestones. Use a prioritization method like the Eisenhower Matrix to determine the urgency and importance of each task.\n\n3. **Estimate time requirements**: Be realistic about how long each task will take.
